| todos-writeback-zh | zh | pass | judge: The result message gives the outcome in short Chinese (typo fixed, two similar fixes, draft PR open and not merged) without claiming a comment was posted or the issue closed. ### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは届いたかを確認して順番どおりに届ける信頼性重視の通信、UDPは確認も順序保証もせず速く軽く送る通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, y **merge** para integrar ramas que ya son públicas o compartidas. Nunca hagas rebase de ramas que otros ya tienen, porque reescribe la historia y les complica el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输：先握手建连，保证数据完整有序地到达；UDP 则不建连、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to keep history linear before you merge it. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, or when you want to keep the real history, and never rebase commits others have already pulled.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/date.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test date.test
```

如果只想跑某个文件里的某一个测试，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按测试名过滤：

```bash
bun test src/utils/date.test.ts -t "formats ISO dates"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover exactly what was committed, so no data is lost or left half-written.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改 compaction 那个 PR 的锁粒度，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"。

要做的：
- 找到 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁改细（缩小临界区，或按粒度拆锁），在你当前的分支上继续做，不要新开分支。
- 行为保持不变：跑一遍相关测试，证明 compaction 的结果没变。
- 完成标准：列出改了哪些地方、测试输出；如果 review 里有几条意见没处理，说明原因。
- 范围外：不合并、不推送、不把 PR 从 draft 改成 ready，也不回复 review 评论。改动只留在分支上。
```

- 改动放在当前分支上提交，报告里附上测量命令和结果。

不在范围内：合并到主分支、推送、开 PR。都等用户点头。

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五的票价已经填进去了，每天早上查到新的就自动更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2419 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="low">还没查</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">北京 → 上海 · 周五</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"><div class="k-empty">还没有票价，每天早上更新。</div></div>
</div>
<p id="updated" style="color:var(--d-muted);font-size:12px;margin-top:8px"></p>
<style>
  .k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px}
  .k-row.dim{opacity:.45}
  .k-row-main{font-weight:600;color:var(--d-ink)}
  .k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px}
  .k-row-value{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;color:var(--d-ink)}
  .k-row-value.good{color:var(--d-good)}
</style>
<script>
  var DEF = {day:"周五",limit:600,updated:"",trains:[{no:"G1",price:553,seats:12},{no:"G3",price:553,seats:4},{no:"G7",price:626,seats:null}]};
  function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c];});}
  function render(d){
    d = d || DEF;
    var limit = d.limit || 600;
    var trains = (d.trains || []).slice().sort(function(a,b){return a.price-b.price;});
    var under = trains.filter(function(t){return t.price < limit;});
    var countEl = document.getElementById("count");
    var lowEl = document.getElementById("low");
    if (trains.length === 0) { countEl.textContent = "还没查"; lowEl.textContent = "每天早上更新"; }
    else {
      countEl.textContent = under.length + " 班";
      lowEl.textContent = under.length ? "最低 " + under[0].price + " 元" : "这周没有 600 以下的";
    }
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    if (!trains.length) return;
    list.innerHTML = trains.map(function(t){
      var ok = t.price < limit;
      var seats = t.seats == null ? "余票未报" : "二等座余 " + t.seats + " 张";
      return '<div class="k-row' + (ok ? "" : " dim") + '">' +

</script>

  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total:"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){

  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

  if (!name || !(amount >= 0)) return;

  l.push({ name, amount, date: $('date').value.trim() });
  await ducter.set('expenses', l);
  $('name').value = ''; $('amt').value = ''; $('date').value = '';
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"name":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amount":662,"date":"11/20"},{"name":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amount":1036,"date":"11/20–21"}]}

  $('list').onclick = function (ev) {
    var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    sel = Number(row.getAttribute('data-id'));
    render();
  };

  $('remove').onclick = function () {
    items = items.filter(function (e) { return e.id !== sel; });
    sel = null;
    save();
    render();
  };

  $('done').onclick = function () {
    sel = null;
    render();
  };

})();

<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">

  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="t">时间</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="如 14:30">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="如 90">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var feeds = [];

  function p(n) { return n < 10 ? '0' + n : '' + n; }
  function today() {
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate());
  }
  function todays() {
    var t = today();
    return feeds.filter(function (f) { return f.date === t; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return a.time < b. …       var idx = feeds.indexOf(f);
          if (idx >= 0) {
            feeds.splice(idx, 1);
            save();
          }
        };
        listEl.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(list.length ? '今天 ' + list.length + ' 次,' + total + ' ml' : '今天还没记');
    }
  }

  function save() {
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    render();
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(function (v) {
      feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  function normTime(s) {
    var m = String(s || '').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2})[:：](\d{2})$/);
    if (!m) return null;
    var h = +m[1], mi = +m[2];
    if (h > 23 || mi > 59) return null;
    return p(h) + ':' + p(mi);
  }

  document.getElementById('save').onclick = function () {
    var t = normTime(document.getElementById('t').value);
    var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!t || !(ml > 0)) return;
    feeds.push({ date: today(), time: t, ml: ml });
    document.getElementById('t').value = '';
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    save();
  };

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 笔记本电脑插画，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，背景是透明或白色的。

适合放在空状态里。空白的屏幕正好表达"这里还没有内容"，柔和的米色和立体质感也不会显得冷冰冰。用的时候注意两点：屏幕空白可能让人以为是加载失败，所以旁边最好配一句说明，比如"还没有内容，点这里新建"；另外确认它的底色跟页面背景搭得上，深色模式下米色可能会显得突兀。
```
